<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (9 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
Create a photorealistic zombie-horror sequence inside a high-security research laboratory. The central character is a young adult Korean female scientist dressed in a clean white lab coat over a simple light-colored shirt, blue straight-leg jeans, and practical white sneakers. She has natural dark hair, minimal makeup, realistic skin texture, and believable proportions. Her face, hairstyle, lab coat, jeans, shoes, and body proportions must remain consistent throughout the entire sequence. Build the environment with steel machinery, specimen tables, glass containment chambers, computer monitors, harsh fluorescent lighting, and realistic laboratory details. Use grounded practical effects and physically believable movement with tense handheld camerawork.

While she works at a containment station, a glass specimen chamber suddenly develops fractures and explodes, scattering glass across the floor as a small dark parasite escapes and attaches itself around her shoulder and neck. She stumbles backward, frightened, while dark veins gradually appear beneath her skin and spread upward across her neck and face. Her skin becomes noticeably pale and clammy, her fingers twitch, and violent tremors run through her body. Her terrified expression gradually disappears into a blank, emotionless stare as her eyes transform into an unnaturally deep obsidian black. She abruptly turns toward a nearby colleague and charges, forcing him behind a glass partition.

She repeatedly strikes the glass while staring directly at him, her completely black eyes and pulsing dark veins clearly visible. Two more scientists enter and freeze when she suddenly becomes perfectly still, then slowly rotates her head toward them. Around the laboratory, additional containment chambers begin cracking as warning lights activate. Another infected creature breaks free, sending the scientists scrambling for emergency equipment. They use a powerful emergency strobe and fire extinguisher to disorient the creatures before pushing a heavy steel cart across the corridor and retreating behind a security door.

The surviving scientists reach an emergency corridor and catch their breath as red warning lights wash over their exhausted faces. The sealed laboratory door violently shakes behind them. The camera gradually approaches its small observation window, revealing the infected woman standing motionless in the darkness, still wearing her white lab coat and blue jeans. Her pale face, prominent dark veins, and completely black eyes are visible through the glass. She slowly tilts her head toward the camera as the emergency lights flicker across her face. Cut to black.

Visual and audio direction: Ultra-realistic sci-fi zombie horror with detailed natural skin, convincing black eyes, subtle veins beneath the skin, realistic facial changes, practical creature effects, authentic anatomy, and believable environmental physics. Maintain cold clinical lighting before transitioning to red emergency illumination. Use handheld camera movement and natural diegetic sounds such as breaking glass, alarms, footsteps, breathing, impacts, convulsions, and distant screams. Avoid graphic gore or excessive blood. No anime, cartoon, illustration, artificial CGI appearance, plastic skin, duplicated characters, face morphing, distorted anatomy, floating objects, impossible movement, changing clothing, identity changes, subtitles, text overlays, logos, or watermark.
```

</details>

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/media/poster/seedance-format-30-seconds-16-9-ultra-photorealistic-live-action-continuous-handh-9260ed6474c5.jpg" width="600" alt="Early Morning Visit to a Fishing Village in Sokcho">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-format-30-seconds-16-9-ultra-photorealistic-live-action-continuous-handh-9260ed6474c5?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**Author:** @oggii_0 | **Source:** [Original](https://x.com/oggii_0/status/2095029762070900919) | **Published:** 2026-09-02 | **Heat:** 75

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (63 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
FORMAT: 30 seconds | 16:9 | Ultra-photorealistic live-action | Continuous handheld vacation vlog

Preserve the exact face, hairstyle, identity, skin tone, facial features, and body proportions from <<<image_1>>> throughout the entire video. The same woman must appear consistently in every shot.

VISUAL STYLE: Authentic late-2000s personal vacation vlog recorded on a cheap handheld flip camera. Extremely raw and imperfect footage. Heavy natural camera shake, awkward handheld framing, occasional partial face crops, sudden reframing, autofocus hunting, exposure fluctuations, blown highlights, slightly soft image quality, warm faded colors, mild digital noise, compression artifacts, and subtle motion blur. No cinematic camera movements, no gimbal stabilization, no polished composition, no modern color grading, no beauty filter, no artificial sharpness.

Outfit: oversized grey hooded sweatshirt, black leggings, worn white running shoes, hair pulled back into a low messy ponytail, small silver stud earrings, a canvas tote bag slung over one shoulder. The entire video must feel like one genuine recording captured by a friend on an early-morning trip through a fishing village in Sokcho, South Korea, not a staged commercial or cinematic recreation.

00:00–00:04 — EARLY MORNING ARRIVAL
The woman walks along a quiet harbor path as the sun rises, fishing boats bobbing gently in the water behind her, seagulls circling overhead.
The camera shakes slightly as it follows her from the side.
She says:
"It's so early, but I really wanted to see the fish market before it gets busy."

00:04–00:08 — THE FISH MARKET
She steps into a covered harbor market filled with tanks of live fish, crabs, and squid, fishermen unloading crates from small boats nearby.
The camera zooms unsteadily on a tank of wriggling crabs, focus hunting before snapping back to her face.
She reacts with a mix of fascination and mild alarm.

00:08–00:12 — TALKING WITH A FISHERMAN
An older fisherman hoists a large fish onto a table for her to see up close.
She leans in curiously, asks a question, and he answers while gesturing with his hands.
She laughs and says to the camera:
"He says this one was caught just an hour ago!"

00:12–00:16 — TRYING RAW SQUID
A nearby stall vendor offers her a small plate of freshly sliced raw squid with dipping sauce.
She hesitates for a second, then takes a bite, chewing slowly with a surprised expression.
She turns to the camera and says:
"Okay wow, that's way fresher than I expected."

00:16–00:20 — HELPING SORT THE CATCH
A fisherwoman nearby is sorting fish into baskets and waves her over to help.
She kneels down and awkwardly tries to sort a few fish, nearly dropping one, laughing at herself.
The fisherwoman laughs too and pats her on the back encouragingly.

00:20–00:24 — WALKING THE HARBOR
She walks along the docks past rows of moored fishing boats, ropes coiled on the ground, nets drying in the morning sun.
A couple of fishermen nod and greet her; she waves back cheerfully.
The camera briefly swings toward the boats before swinging back to her.

00:24–00:27 — QUIET MOMENT
She stops at the edge of the harbor, looking out over the calm water as the sun fully rises over the horizon.
For a moment there's no dialogue — just waves lapping, distant gulls, and the creak of boats.
She smiles softly and says:
"Mornings like this make the early wake-up worth it."

00:27–00:30 — NATURAL ENDING
She turns back toward the camera, gives a small wave, and says:
"Okay, harbor adventure done. See you next time!"
She laughs, turns, and walks off along the dock.
The camera lingers a moment before the footage naturally ends with a slight shake and imperfect cut.

AUDIO
Natural location sound only:
- waves lapping against the dock
- seagulls calling
- boat engines idling in the distance
- fishermen calling out to each other
- crates being unloaded
- water splashing in fish tanks
- footsteps on wet pavement
- light morning breeze
```

</details>

Her flowing silk sleeves and long hair move naturally with her motion. Smooth camera rotation, realistic sword choreography, cinematic motion blur.

Visual Style: Photorealistic Chinese historical fantasy, premium Wuxia movie cinematography, dark bamboo forest, cold teal and muted green color grading, soft mist, volumetric lighting, realistic heavy rain, detailed wet silk fabric, natural human anatomy, realistic facial expressions, shallow depth of field, 4K cinematic quality, 24fps, dramatic atmosphere.

Camera: Low-angle tracking shots, slow dolly-in, extreme facial close-ups, overhead action shots, dynamic tracking movements, smooth cinematic transitions, controlled slow motion.

Audio: Heavy rainfall, footsteps on wet mud, metallic sword clashes, sharp weapon whooshes, flowing fabric sounds, subtle tense orchestral background music.

Consistency: Maintain the same woman's face, hairstyle, pale mint-green Hanfu outfit, and body proportions throughout the video. Keep all warriors dressed in black traditional assassin clothing. Natural physics, coherent action, realistic weapon movements.

Negative Prompt: Cartoon, anime, CGI appearance, plastic skin, blurry faces, distorted anatomy, extra fingers, extra limbs, duplicate characters, floating weapons, unrealistic fighting, inconsistent clothing, face morphing, flickering, low resolution, oversaturated colors, text, subtitles, watermark.

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (52 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
CHARACTER:
A young woman wearing a cute, stylish amusement-park outfit with a clearly visible Mickey Mouse ears headband. Keep the headband consistent throughout the entire video. She is excited, playful, and expressive.
A cheerful Turkish ice cream vendor stands behind a traditional Turkish ice cream stall, holding a long metal ice cream paddle.

0–3 SEC — ORDER
Start with a cinematic wide shot of the colorful amusement park, then smoothly move toward the Turkish ice cream stall.

The girl approaches the counter, smiling excitedly. Her Mickey Mouse ears headband is clearly visible. She points toward the ice cream.
The vendor prepares a cone with a scoop of ice cream and extends it toward her.

3–6 SEC — THE FIRST TEASE
Cut to a medium shot.

The girl eagerly reaches for the cone.

Just as she is about to grab it, the vendor smoothly pulls the ice cream away with the long paddle.
She looks at him in surprise.

He smiles mischievously and offers it again.

6–9 SEC — MORE TEASING
She reaches again.

The vendor smoothly moves the cone from left to right, then slightly upward, always just out of reach.

The girl follows the cone, laughing and becoming increasingly determined.

Keep the interaction fluid, realistic, and playful, with natural body movement and timing.
9–11 SEC — THE NOSE TRICK
The vendor finally brings the ice cream toward her.

She confidently leans forward, expecting to receive it.

Instead, he gently places a small dab of ice cream on the tip of her nose.

Close-up on her shocked expression.

She pauses, looks cross-eyed toward the ice cream on her nose, then looks back at the vendor with a playful annoyed expression.
11–15 SEC — SHE WINS
Before the vendor can tease her again, she quickly grabs the cone from the paddle.

She successfully gets the ice cream.

The vendor laughs and raises his hands in playful defeat.

She proudly holds up the cone, smiles toward the camera, wipes the ice cream from her nose, and takes a happy bite.

CINEMATOGRAPHY:
Photorealistic live-action, premium cinematic quality, warm golden-hour lighting, natural skin texture, realistic facial expressions, shallow depth of field, soft background bokeh, subtle anamorphic lens characteristics, natural motion blur, smooth gimbal movement, cinematic push-ins and tracking shots, realistic amusement-park atmosphere.

IMPORTANT:
Keep the Mickey Mouse ears headband clearly visible and consistent throughout the scene. Maintain consistent faces, wardrobe, hairstyle, ice cream cone, vendor paddle, and environment. All movements must be smooth and physically realistic. No exaggerated cartoon movement, no 3D animation, no jerky motion, no teleporting, no object duplication, and no unnatural hand movements.
```

</details>

```
CRIMSON FLASHPOINT — 48 seconds, 16:9, 12 shots × 4 seconds. Photorealistic Hollywood superhero action film, ARRI ALEXA 65, anamorphic look, subtle film grain.

LOCATION: Rain-soaked neon downtown intersection at night, glass skyscrapers, abstract cyan/magenta/amber signs, reflective wet asphalt, steam grates, parked cars, dark alley on the right. Heavy rain throughout; crimson is the dominant accent.

HERO (@image1): Preserve identical face, hair, beard, skin tone, age, and build in every shot. Original outfit before transformation; afterward, matte-black articulated armor with glowing crimson seams across ribs, arms, legs, and spine, segmented chest plate with a faint red core, fingerless armored gloves. No helmet or cape; face fully visible.

ENEMIES: Exactly three masked men in black tactical jackets, cargo pants, grey gloves, and matte-black full-face masks. Man 1 is tall and lean with a steel baton; Man 2 is broad with a crowbar; Man 3 has a medium build and baton. No dialogue.

SHOT 01 (00–04): Extreme-wide low-angle skyline under storm clouds. A bright crimson streak cuts diagonally through the sky, illuminating rain and glass towers.

SHOT 02 (04–08): Fast overhead tracking follows the falling streak toward street level. A faint human silhouette appears inside the light as crimson reflections sweep across the road.

SHOT 03 (08–12): Hero lands in a one-knee, one-fist crouch in his original outfit. Water explodes outward; red energy threads crawl across his arms and torso.

SHOT 04 (12–16): Smooth 360° orbit as crimson energy assembles the black armor panel by panel. The chest plate locks into place and red seams ignite.

SHOT 05 (16–20): 85mm close-up. Rain beads on his face and armor; a red spark flickers in his eyes as he looks forward with controlled determination.

SHOT 06 (20–24): Low side-tracking shot. Fully armored hero sprints down the wet street at superhuman speed, spraying water and leaving a thin crimson trail.

SHOT 07 (24–28): Over-the-shoulder shot. Three masked men emerge from the right-side alley, spreading out with weapons ready. Hero skids to a defensive stop.

SHOT 08 (28–32): Hero dashes forward as a crimson blur and strikes Man 1, launching him backward. Rain and water burst outward in slow motion.

SHOT 09 (32–36): Man 2 swings his crowbar. Hero ducks, spins, and lands one strike, sending him sliding through a puddle toward a parked car.

SHOT 10 (36–40): Man 3 lunges with his baton. Hero dodges inches away and delivers a powerful palm strike, blasting him backward through an explosive ring of water. Bright crimson flash at impact.

SHOT 11 (40–44): Wide low-angle pull-back. Hero stands calmly between all three fallen attackers as rain pours over his armor and crimson seams gradually dim.

SHOT 12 (44–48): Extreme close-up of the hero lifting his head with quiet confidence. A tiny red spark crosses his eyes before the image cuts to black.

AUDIO: Original cinematic orchestral-synth score, heavy rain, electrical crackle, realistic impacts, water splashes, accelerating percussion, and a powerful musical peak at Shot 10. No dialogue.

CONTINUITY: Maintain exact hero identity, armor design, enemy count, location layout, lighting, and screen direction across all shots. Realistic physics, consistent rain, natural motion blur, controlled speed ramps, clean cuts, and stable camera movement.

NEGATIVE: Cartoon, artificial CGI appearance, plastic skin, face changes, inconsistent armor, extra characters, duplicate bodies, extra fingers, deformed hands, cape, helmet, yellow accents, lightning-bolt emblem, blood, gore, impossible physics, random camera movement, jitter, flicker, readable text, logos, or watermark.

Shot 1 (0–1.2s): Character A, matching reference face/outfit, sits beside a passenger on a dim train, feverish, sweating, staring strangely at him.

Shot 2 (1.2–2.4s): Close-up: her trembling hand, faint dark veins appearing, shallow unnatural breathing.

Shot 3 (2.4–3.6s): Her head tilts unnaturally toward him, glassy unfocused eyes, tense profile close-up.

Shot 4 (3.6–4.8s): She suddenly lunges and bites his forearm, teeth breaking skin, his face frozen in shock.

Shot 5 (4.8–6s): Close-up of the wound as dark veins rapidly branch outward, skin blackening.

Shot 6 (6–7.2s): Veins race toward his elbow; skin pales, sweat beads on his forehead.

Shot 7 (7.2–8.5s): His eyes cloud milky white, pupils dilate, breathing becomes wet and ragged.

Shot 8 (8.5–9.7s): His body stiffens and twitches; black veins spread across his neck and jaw.

Shot 9 (9.7–11s): He throws his head back and screams inhumanly, face covered in black veins, slow motion.

Shot 10 (11–12.2s): His head snaps forward; bloodshot empty eyes and slightly unhinged jaw reveal the completed transformation.

Shot 11 (12.2–13.5s): He instantly lunges at another passenger and bites them, sudden violent impact.

Shot 12 (13.5–14.8s): Wider shot: nearby passengers turn, confusion becoming horror.

Shot 13 (14.8–16s): The bitten passenger stumbles back, clutching the wound as dark veins spread up the arm.

Shot 14 (16–17.3s): Passengers rise in panic, grabbing bags and backing away, wide cinematic shot.

Shot 15 (17.3–18.5s): The new victim convulses violently in the aisle as passengers recoil; overhead lights flicker.

Shot 16 (18.5–19.7s): Eyes roll back, then open bloodshot and empty; jaw twitches—the second transformation complete.

Shot 17 (19.7–21s): He lunges at the nearest passenger; screams erupt as the car descends into chaos, handheld camera.

Shot 18 (21–22.5s): Passengers scramble over seats and luggage toward the exit, bags spilling everywhere.

Shot 19 (22.5–24s): A group rushes toward the connecting door, looking back as infection spreads behind them.

Shot 20 (24–25.5s): Door slams shut; survivors barricade it with luggage and cushions as infected hands pound from behind.

Shot 21 (25.5–27.5s): Close-up through the glass: infected faces press against it as cracks spread, emergency lights pulsing.

Shot 22 (27.5–30s): Survivors huddle against the far wall, breathing hard and staring at the shaking door as train lights streak outside, cinematic outbreak ending.

### POV Cliff Jump into a Turquoise Volcanic Canyon Spring

> omg...Seedance 2.5 can do real POV physics now one prompt on @FishCreativeHQ.. bookmark this 👇 Prompt: SEEDANCE 2.5 | TEXT TO VIDEO | SINGLE CONTINUOUS TAKE | …

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/ba78b17e3025.jpg" width="600" alt="POV Cliff Jump into a Turquoise Volcanic Canyon Spring">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-a-hidden-volcanic-spring-canyon-deep-in-a-pacific-northwest-pine-forest-on-a-br-ff48a4636c95?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**Author:** @abxxai | **Source:** [Original](https://x.com/abxxai/status/2103861170625286334) | **Published:** 2026-09-26 | **Heat:** 73

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (50 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
SEEDANCE 2.5 | TEXT TO VIDEO | SINGLE CONTINUOUS TAKE | 22s | 1080x1920 | 9:16 | 30fps | 1080p

SCENE AND LOCATION
A hidden volcanic spring canyon deep in a Pacific Northwest pine forest on a bright summer day. A narrow, deep gorge with steep, very high walls of dark gray basalt made of vertical hexagonal columns, streaked with tan and rust tones and covered in patches of bright green moss, ferns and small shrubs.
At the bottom is a crystal-clear natural spring pool. The shallow edges glow bright turquoise and aqua, fading to deep sapphire blue in the center. The water is so transparent that the bottom is fully visible: honeycomb-patterned volcanic rock, mossy boulders, and two long pale submerged logs lying diagonally across the bottom.
Two white waterfalls pour into the pool: a thin one at the far back of the canyon and a larger one spilling from the right-hand cliff wall, making white foam where it hits the water. Tall dark-green conifer and pine trees line the canyon rim. Above is a vivid clear blue sky with a few small white clouds. Hard, bright midday sunlight with strong contrast, and sun sparkles on the water.

THE JUMPER
A young man seen only from his own first-person view. Tanned bare feet and lightly hairy shins, and loose navy-blue swim shorts with a large mustard-yellow hibiscus flower pattern and white drawstrings. No shirt, no shoes, no gloves. His hands and forearms appear naturally in frame when he moves them.

CAMERA AND REALISM
First-person POV from a GoPro strapped to his forehead. It looks like real action-cam footage, not a cinematic camera. 16mm-equivalent ultra-wide lens with slight barrel distortion, and saturated but natural color.
Use real action-cam behavior throughout: small head bob and micro-jitter from breathing and body weight, rolling-shutter wobble on fast moves, quick auto-exposure shifts between bright sky and the shaded canyon, a small sun flare on upward tilts, light sensor noise in the shadows, and wind buffeting that shakes the frame during the fall.
The cliff is about 35 meters high. Gravity, body motion and water physics are fully realistic.

0-2s | THE EDGE (real time)
POV looking straight down from the top of the basalt cliff. His bare feet stand on the rough, dark rock ledge at the bottom of frame, with the shorts visible at the lower edge. Both hands hover forward at the sides of frame. The turquoise pool, the submerged logs and the waterfalls are far, far below. His toes curl over the edge and small grit crumbles off. His right hand tosses a pink-and-white striped inflatable tube out over the drop; it tumbles down and lands as a tiny speck on the water far below. Wind, distant waterfall roar, upbeat chill electronic music starts.

2-4s | THE COUNTDOWN (real time)
A quick nervous tilt down at the drop, a sharp exhale, then two fast steps back from the edge, with the ledge bouncing in frame. Young male voice, hyped: "Three, two, one!"

4-7s | THE TAKEOFF (natural slow motion, about 50% speed)
Two running steps forward, then his last foot pushes off the ledge in slow motion. Rock grit kicks up and floats in the sunlight, the cliff edge slides away beneath his feet, his arms swing forward and out wide, and the whole turquoise canyon opens up below. The slow motion looks like a real high-frame-rate action-cam clip, not a frozen hang. Deep bass swell, stretched wind.

7-13s | THE FALL (real time, fast and continuous)
The speed ramps smoothly back to normal and the stomach drops.
7-9s: The frame dips and wobbles, his arms flail slightly for balance at the edges of frame, and his feet kick at the bottom of frame. A loud yell: "Woooooo!"
9-11s: The basalt column wall, moss and ferns streak upward past the lens, and the right-side waterfall rushes by with spray catching the sun. Wind roars louder and the frame shakes from air buffeting.
11-13s: The glowing turquoise water rushes up and fills the frame, and the pink tube and submerged logs grow fast. His arms snap tight to his body and his legs lock straight for a feet-first pencil dive. The music builds to the drop.

13-14s | IMPACT
A hard feet-first hit into the water and a white explosion of bubbles swallows the lens. Loud plunge splash, deep impact whoosh, and the music goes heavily muffled underwater.

14-17s | UNDERWATER (short, energetic, real time)
A violent swirl of silver bubbles clears to reveal crystal-clear blue water, bright sun shafts cutting down from the surface, the pale submerged logs and honeycomb volcanic rock bottom below, and small fish darting away. His hands push into frame for one strong breaststroke pull, then fast kicks straight up toward the bright glowing surface, with bubbles streaming past the lens. No slow sinking.

17-19s | SURFACE BREAK
The camera bursts through the surface, water droplets sliding across the lens, and bright sunlight floods in. The music snaps back to full clarity on the beat drop. His hand grabs the pink-and-white tube floating right beside him.

19-22s | THE REWARD
POV bobbing on the turquoise water holding the tube, then a sharp tilt up revealing the towering basalt cliff, the tiny ledge he jumped from far above, the pine trees on the rim and the vivid blue sky, with the waterfall splashing on the right. Same voice, out of breath and triumphant: "Yeah, dude! That was insane!" Faint distant cheering. Music plays clean to the final frame.

AUDIO
Continuous upbeat chill electronic track: a bass swell on takeoff, a fast build through the fall, muffled underwater, then a clean drop on surfacing. Real ambient layers: wind that grows with speed, fabric flapping, heavy breathing, waterfall roar, splash, bubbles. Dialogue clear over the music.

KEEP
One continuous take, first-person only. The same shorts, feet and hands throughout, and the same canyon layout with two waterfalls, turquoise pool and submerged logs. The slow motion lasts only 3 seconds at takeoff. The fall is fast, real time and builds speed without pausing. Every beat is quick and punchy.

AVOID
Extra slow motion beyond the takeoff, hovering or floating mid-air, smooth cinematic gimbal movement, cuts to third person, showing the jumper's face, extra people, distorted hands or extra fingers, morphing cliffs, changing water color, text, subtitles, watermarks.
```

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (80 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic Hollywood-level cinematic thriller scene titled “THE LAST CALL.” The entire sequence must feel like a scene from a high-budget live-action feature film, with realistic human acting, natural facial expressions, physically accurate movement, realistic rain, practical lighting, believable vehicle physics, detailed environments, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, natural skin texture, realistic reflections, and professional Hollywood cinematography. No anime, no CGI-looking visuals, no fantasy aesthetic, no cyberpunk, no neon lighting, no exaggerated visual effects.
MAIN CHARACTER: A realistic young woman in her late 20s, natural appearance, expressive eyes, shoulder-length dark hair slightly damp from the humid weather, wearing a simple dark jacket. Maintain the exact same woman, face, hairstyle, clothing, car, weather and environment consistently throughout the entire sequence.

SETTING: A remote mountain road at night during heavy but realistic rainfall. Dark forested mountains surround the narrow winding road. Wet asphalt reflects only natural headlights and road markings. The atmosphere is tense, isolated and believable.

0–5 SECONDS

Interior of a moving car at night. Medium shot from the passenger side showing the woman driving alone. Rain continuously hits the windshield and realistic windshield wipers move back and forth. Dashboard illumination softly lights her face. She looks tired and emotionally distant while concentrating on the road. Camera slowly pushes toward her face. Natural breathing, subtle blinking and realistic micro-expressions.

5–10 SECONDS

Her smartphone suddenly begins ringing in the center console. Cut to an extremely realistic close-up of the phone screen.

Caller ID: “MOM”

The woman freezes.

Her eyes widen slightly. Her hands tighten around the steering wheel. She looks at the phone in disbelief. She hesitates for a moment, then reaches down and answers the call.

10–17 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her face as she listens.

A faint, emotional female voice comes through the phone:

“Don't take the next turn.”

The woman's expression changes from confusion to fear.

The call suddenly disconnects.

She looks at the phone.

The screen reads: CALL ENDED

Outside the windshield, a sharp mountain turn approaches through the heavy rain.

17–23 SECONDS

She immediately slows the car.

Camera moves to a forward-facing perspective through the windshield. The road curves sharply ahead.

Suddenly, distant headlights appear through the rain around the corner.

The woman looks toward them.

A large truck suddenly loses control and crashes through the roadside barrier at the exact location where her car would have been if she had continued driving.

The crash must look physically realistic and grounded, with believable vehicle momentum, rain, debris and impact. No exaggerated explosions.

23–27 SECONDS

Silence after the crash.

The woman sits completely still, breathing heavily, visibly shaken.

She slowly looks down at her phone.

The phone screen now shows:

NO SIM — NO SIGNAL

She looks confused and terrified.

27–30 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her eyes filling with tears.

She looks toward the empty passenger seat.

A subtle emotional pause.

In a trembling whisper, she says:

“Mom?”

Cut instantly to black.

FINAL AUDIO: Heavy rain, windshield wipers, realistic car interior ambience, distant thunder, phone ringing, muffled female voice, sudden silence after the crash, then only the woman's breathing.
```

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (116 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
One door. Five worlds. Endless possibilities. Where would you go? ✨🌍

made with Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI .
 
Here is the prompt🔽🔽🔽

Create a 28-second ultra-cinematic surreal fashion adventure starring my female AI model. Preserve her EXACT facial identity, facial structure, hairstyle, skin tone, body proportions and recognizable appearance throughout the entire video.

The film must feel like ONE continuous impossible journey, not separate AI-generated clips. Photorealistic, mysterious, premium and visually spectacular.

0–4 SEC — THE MYSTERIOUS HOTEL

Begin with an extreme cinematic close-up of my model's eyes.

Camera smoothly pulls backward, revealing her standing inside an impossibly long, luxurious old-European hotel corridor at night.

Warm golden wall lamps illuminate hundreds of identical doors stretching into the distance.

She wears an elegant flowing black evening dress with subtle jewellery.

She looks toward one particular door and confidently approaches it.

CAMERA: Slow dolly backward → smooth over-the-shoulder orbit as her hand reaches toward the brass handle.

She opens the door.

Intense white light floods through it and completely fills the lens.

SEAMLESS TRANSITION THROUGH THE LIGHT.

4–9 SEC — STORMY ICELAND

The camera passes through the doorway directly behind her.

Without stopping her walking motion, she emerges onto a spectacular Icelandic black-sand beach.

Towering dark cliffs, crashing ocean waves, mist and light rain surround her.

Strong wind naturally moves her hair and black dress.

CAMERA: Begin low beside her feet → rapidly rise into a cinematic tracking orbit around her.

Standing completely alone on the beach is another mysterious hotel door.

She reaches it and immediately opens it.

Camera follows directly through.

9–14 SEC — FUTURISTIC TOKYO

She steps through the same doorway directly into a futuristic Tokyo street at midnight.

Her walking movement continues seamlessly.

Wet streets reflect enormous neon signs, futuristic architecture and passing autonomous vehicles.

Crowds naturally move around her while she confidently walks toward camera.

CAMERA: 35mm anamorphic backward tracking shot → subtle handheld micro-movement → gentle push toward her face.

A sleek futuristic motorcycle suddenly races extremely close across the foreground.

For a fraction of a second, the motorcycle completely covers the camera.

USE THE MOTORCYCLE AS THE TRANSITION.

14–19 SEC — UNDERWATER BALLROOM

As the motorcycle clears the lens, she is suddenly walking through an enormous abandoned luxury ballroom deep underwater.

She does NOT wear diving equipment.

She walks normally while her dress and hair gently react to the surrounding water.

Gigantic floor-to-ceiling windows reveal the deep ocean outside.

A huge whale silently glides past the windows behind her.

Blue sunlight beams penetrate through the water while thousands of tiny particles float naturally through the scene.

CAMERA: Slow cinematic 180° orbit around her as she turns slightly toward the whale.

Suddenly hundreds of shimmering silver fish rush directly toward and across the camera.

They completely fill the frame.

19–23 SEC — GOLDEN DESERT

The silver fish seamlessly transform into thousands of glowing golden particles carried by desert wind.

Camera pushes through the particles.

Reveal my model walking along the crest of an enormous golden sand dune during sunset.

Her outfit has seamlessly transformed into an elegant flowing ivory desert outfit.

HER FACE AND IDENTITY MUST REMAIN EXACTLY THE SAME.

Perfect AI cinematography is overrated.
Sometimes you need shaky tracking, missed framing, ugly zooms and compression artifacts to make something feel REAL. 👀

This Seedance 2.5 experiment proves it.
Full prompt below 👇
Ultra-realistic viral helicopter pursuit footage, raw local-news style, filmed entirely from one helicopter in bright Los Angeles daylight. Long-lens aerial camera, unstable vibration, abrupt zooms, compression artifacts, heat haze, autofocus breathing, imperfect framing, rotor thump, distant radio chatter, harsh California sun, no cinematic polish, believable amateur-news realism.

Main subject: one motocross rider on a dirt bike, full helmet and motocross gear, highly skilled, aggressive but controlled riding. Keep the same rider and same bike throughout.

Los Angeles daytime: palm-lined boulevard, parking lots, freeway ramps, concrete flood channel, industrial blocks, overpasses, service roads and distant skyline.

0-5s: Immediate viral hook The helicopter camera is already searching frantically when the rider suddenly blasts into frame between moving cars. Operator snaps into a shaky zoom. The rider jumps a curb, cuts across a parking lot and nearly disappears behind palm trees.

5-10s: Chaotic tracking He rejoins a boulevard, threads through traffic, then veers up a sloped driveway and launches over a low divider. Camera over-zooms, loses him for half a second, then catches him landing cleanly.

10-15s: Escalation The rider enters a concrete flood channel. Huge aerial reveal. He accelerates along the channel floor while the helicopter banks hard overhead. Dust trails behind the bike. Quick zoom on spinning rear wheel and suspension.

15-20s: Viral stunt beat He rides up the angled concrete wall, holds a dramatic high line for a moment, then drops back down smoothly. Camera shakes violently trying to follow. Rotor vibration and radio chatter intensify.

20-25s: Big surprise The rider exits through a service ramp, crosses beneath an overpass, then unexpectedly appears on a parallel industrial road. The operator pans late and barely recovers him. He hits a natural rise and gets long airtime in full daylight.

25-30s: Iconic ending He lands, accelerates toward another overpass and vanishes into shadow underneath. The helicopter overshoots, circles, zooms wildly across multiple roads, but he is gone. Reporter voice, stunned: “We just lost him.” Hold on shaky wide aerial of Los Angeles. Hard cut.

One continuous helicopter perspective, strong amateur-news authenticity, same rider throughout, realistic motocross physics, believable Los Angeles geography, harsh daylight, imperfect tracking, viral unpredictability, readable stunts, no crash, no gore, no readable text, no logos.

DURATION: 15 seconds | ASPECT RATIO: 16:9

STYLE: Ultra-realistic premium Japanese drama, cinematic film quality

IDENTITY LOCK:
The woman @image1 must keep the exact same face, face shape, skin tone, hairstyle, height, body proportions, and wardrobe in every shot. The man @image2 must keep his exact face, hairstyle, and outfit as in the reference. No changes in any frame.

ENVIRONMENT LOCK:
An elegant Edo-era Japanese shrine courtyard with traditional wooden architecture, tiled rooftops, stone lanterns, and a raked gravel garden in the background. A giant ancient plum (ume) tree with dense white and pink blossoms. Its thick main branch extends horizontally above the woman's head. Soft spring daylight.

SHOT BREAKDOWN:

0.0–4.0s | Medium shot
The woman stands beneath the huge horizontal branch. Blossom petals drift in the breeze. She looks up curiously, then raises both hands and shakes the branch. The branch trembles and a shower of petals falls. A lacquered cosmetic box tumbles down through the blossoms and she catches it with both hands. Close-up of her face as she peeks inside. Flat, unimpressed expression. She tosses it casually out of frame.

4.0–7.5s | Medium shot
Her curiosity grows. She shakes the branch harder and petals pour down like rain. A scatter of gold koban coins and a few silk pouches fall. She catches one pouch, raises a single eyebrow, looks bored, and tosses it away.

7.5–10.5s | Slow motion
Determined now, she grips the branch with both hands and shakes with all her strength. The branch shudders, a heavy blossom storm rains down, and the man @image2 suddenly drops from the branch. She gasps, spreads her arms, and catches him just before he hits the ground. Petals swirl around them.

10.5–13.0s | Close-up, 50mm
She looks at his face. Her eyes sparkle and a wide, delighted smile appears. He gives a charming, slightly embarrassed smile and looks back at her.

13.0–15.0s | Medium-wide shot
They turn to the camera together and make small heart shapes with their hands, laughing happily. They turn to each other, laugh, and walk out of frame side by side while petals keep falling behind them. Fade out on the falling blossoms.

EMOTION ARC:
Curious → Bored → More curious → Surprised → Smitten → Happy.

SOUND:
Soft spring breeze, rustling blossoms, creaking wood, light thud of the box, jingle of coins, warm playful Japanese rom-com music (koto, light shamisen, soft piano), cheerful laughter, fading footsteps.

CAMERA & VISUALS:
ARRI Alexa 35 look, 35mm and 50mm cinematic lenses, smooth gimbal movement, slow-motion petal shower, natural spring lighting, realistic skin pores, physically accurate shadows, HDR, subtle film grain, soft pastel color grading.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
cartoon, anime, CGI look, low quality, blurry face, extra fingers, warped hands, duplicate objects, duplicate people, distorted body, floating objects, unrealistic physics, unnatural falling motion, overexposed lighting, oversaturated colors, AI artifacts, camera shake, abrupt cuts, warped architecture, deformed eyes

Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic early-2000s DV home video of a young Korean woman doing quiet Sunday chores around her home in an older Seoul neighborhood. Purely observational, no dialogue, no narrative arc  just rhythmic, textural everyday realism.
MAIN SUBJECT
Young Korean woman same as , early 20s, natural beauty, realistic skin texture, no makeup, black hair tied up messily in a claw clip, oversized faded T-shirt, rolled-up sweatpants, bare feet or house slippers. Keep her identity, outfit, hairstyle and proportions perfectly consistent throughout.
SETTING
The front step and small yard/alley outside an older Seoul home. Concrete step, a low gate, potted plants, a garden hose, a trash bin, scattered leaves. Ordinary, lived-in, no landmarks or brand names.
SEQUENCE (rhythmic, no dialogue)
She sweeps the front step with a short broom, dust and leaves scattering in the light.
She crouches down and pours a small amount of dry food into a bowl for a neighborhood cat waiting nearby; the cat eats while she watches briefly.
She stands and waters potted plants along the wall with a small watering can, water visibly darkening the soil.
She picks up a bagged trash bundle and carries it a few steps to place it by the curb/bin.
She wipes her hands on her shirt, looks around the now-tidy step and yard for a moment, then exhales/stretches.
She glances toward the camera briefly with a small, tired smile — no words — then turns and walks back inside.
Camera holds on the empty, swept step for a beat before cutting to black.
CAMERA
Raw handheld early-2000s DV camcorder, shaky footage, autofocus hunting, faded colors, exposure shifts, motion blur, soft digital detail, occasional accidental zooms. Observational, documentary-like framing — lingering shots, no cuts within actions. No polished cinematography.
AUDIO
Natural ambience only — broom bristles on concrete, water pouring, cat eating, distant birds, wind, a far-off scooter or dog bark, plastic bag rustling, faint footsteps. No dialogue, no music.
CONTINUITY
Broom, watering can and trash bag each remain consistent through their respective actions. Cat stays in frame while eating, doesn't disappear or duplicate. No teleporting or vanishing objects. Smooth, realistic, unhurried movement throughout.
NEGATIVE
CGI look, plastic skin, identity drift, outfit changes, distorted hands, extra fingers, subtitles, watermark.
```

**00:05–00:08** Low front-quarter tracking, 28mm. The Mercedes slips through a gap between a bus and a box truck by inches, then whip pan.

**00:16–00:18** Wide crane-style rise, 24mm. The Mercedes bursts onto an open highway, the SUV closing in.

**00:18–00:20** Interior insert, 35mm. He steers with his left hand and grabs the rifle from the passenger seat with his right.

**00:20–00:23** Over-the-shoulder, 50mm Steadicam. He leans out of the window and aims at the SUV's front tire, with a single muzzle flash.

**00:23–00:26** SLOW MOTION, low side angle, 35mm. The tire bursts and the SUV flips and rolls across the highway in sparks and dust, fully visible. Mercedes sharp in the foreground.

**00:26–00:28** Close-up, 85mm, slow push-in. @image1 hero gives a slow, confident victory smile and a small nod.

**00:28–00:30** High-angle drone rise, 24mm. The Mercedes speeds toward the sunset, smoke rising from the wreck far behind. Fade out.

**AUDIO:** Realistic engines, tire squeal, wind and impacts. Dark hybrid orchestral score with bass pulse, cellos and percussion building to a triumphant finish. Near-silence during both slow-motion crashes.

**NEGATIVE:** No cartoon look, no CGI look, no plastic skin, no distorted anatomy, no face different from @image1, no face drift, no wardrobe change, no duplicated cars, no teleporting vehicles, no hidden or cropped crash, no impossible physics, no jitter, no flickering, no deformed hands or rifle, no text, no watermark, no logos.

The camera should move dynamically with fast tracking shots, low-angle shots, side tracking shots and occasional wide shots revealing the scale of the flooding.

At one point, show the woman jumping over obstacles and running across a market counter/raised surface while the floodwater continues rushing behind her. Keep her appearance, hairstyle, pink outfit and facial features consistent throughout the entire sequence.

Dark cinematic atmosphere, overcast rainy lighting, cool blue-gray tones mixed with warm fluorescent market lights, dramatic contrast, realistic skin texture, photorealistic environment, volumetric lighting, atmospheric mist, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, high-detail textures, realistic reflections and physically accurate water simulation.

The sequence should feel like a high-budget survival/action movie, extremely immersive and intense, with realistic camera shake and fast-paced movement.

Add large cinematic white brush-stroke typography reading “RUN” briefly across the center of the screen during the action sequence.

Photorealistic, ultra-detailed, cinematic Hollywood-style action scene, realistic physics, natural motion, 4K film quality, anamorphic lens look, dynamic composition, dramatic pacing, no cartoon look, no CGI appearance, no distorted anatomy, no extra fingers, no duplicated people, no face changes.

21–26s — Preparing the snack

She sits at the kitchen counter.

Using a small kitchen knife, she carefully slices the peach in half.

The knife meets realistic resistance before passing through the soft fruit.

She removes the pit, cuts several thin slices, and arranges them casually on the plate.

Close-up of realistic juice and peach texture.

26–30s — First bite

She pauses for a moment, smiles naturally, and quietly says:

“So good.”

She looks toward the window as warm sunlight falls across the kitchen.

The camera slowly drifts backward, capturing the cozy kitchen, open window, gentle curtain movement, and late-summer atmosphere.

Cut naturally at the end.

Use the attached image as the character reference and keep her face and appearance consistent. She wears a pink fitted crop top, white casual shorts, simple slippers, and a small bracelet, carrying a clear water bottle.

She steps out of her home, closes the door and walks through the quiet neighborhood with the bottle in hand. Along the way, she greets a few friendly neighbors with natural smiles and small waves.

She reaches a neighborhood park and sits on a bench, casually watching children playing football. Suddenly, the football rolls toward her. She picks it up, smiles, kicks it gently back to the children and says, “Enjoy!” The children laugh and continue playing.

She leaves the park and walks down the street, still carrying her water bottle. She stops at a small street vendor selling sweet corn, buys one and happily takes her first bite while walking.

While eating, her water bottle accidentally slips from her hand and falls onto the sidewalk. She pauses, looks down, laughs softly, picks it up, takes another bite of the sweet corn and continues walking home.

Near the end, she walks slowly down the residential lane with the water bottle in one hand and sweet corn in the other, enjoying her snack. She looks toward the camera, smiles naturally and says “Bye bye!”, then walks away.

Use raw early-2000s consumer DV-camera footage: handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, slight exposure shifts, soft digital detail, mild digital noise, natural motion blur and occasional awkward zooms. Authentic home-video feeling, realistic skin, natural expressions and believable movement.

Natural neighborhood audio only: footsteps, birds, distant traffic, children playing football, neighbors talking, bicycle sounds, vendor ambience, water bottle movement and light wind. No music, narration, subtitles, beauty filters, CGI, cinematic commercial look or polished camera movement.

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (77 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
⤵️ 
SCENE 1 (0:00–0:30) — MONOCHROME STUDIO → FULL RITUAL → EMERALD GOWN → LAST DETAIL
SHOT STRUCTURE: 15 shots, 30 seconds, 16:9
@Image1 is the Aiko character reference — keep her face, fair skin, sleek black bob haircut and dark-brown eyes identical in every shot.
GLOBAL STYLE NOTES:
lighting_philosophy: crisp white beauty-dish key on her face, cool silver glow from square-framed vanity mirrors behind, soft grey practicals on walls; glossy speculars on marble, chrome and glass.
color_grade: high-gloss monochrome beauty commercial — charcoal-grey walls, ivory marble, fair skin held clean and cool; the emerald gown is the only deep saturated green in the film.
setting: sleek minimalist glam studio — charcoal-grey walls, polished ivory marble floor, low velvet stools in ash-grey, white sheepskin rugs, three large square chrome-framed vanity mirrors, a thin white LED strip along the ceiling, beauty-dish lights on stands, a disco ball throwing slow silver dots across the marble, open shelves of matte black labelled bottles and jars, a single orchid in a glass vase, a chrome drinks cart.
staff: three women in charcoal uniforms — an esthetician, a make-up artist, a hair stylist. Shown mainly as hands, forearms, backs and soft-focus bodies. No close-ups of their faces except the final reaction in medium shot. They move fast and react openly.
mirror_rule: mirrors stay BEHIND or BESIDE her, soft-focus, as light and texture only. Never a sharp reflection of her face shown next to her actual face in the same frame.
characters:
Aiko: visual_anchors: petite elegant build, sleek jet-black bob haircut with blunt bangs, dark-brown eyes, fair even skin, a small mole near one eye. Opens in a plain white ribbed tank top. Performance: composed, quietly amused — one mouth corner lifts, never a wide expression. She is the only still person in a room full of motion.
ambience: glossy pop beat locked to every cut, studio chatter under it, hairdryer hum. Beat drops at shot 12, cuts to silence at shot 15.
SEQUENCE LIST:
SHOT 1 (0–2s) HOOK — WS, glass door
camera_motion: static, then fast push-in
action_visual: Aiko (per @Image1) pushes the glass door open in a plain white tank top — cool silver light floods the frame. Door chime, beat starts on her step
exit: she walks past camera into the studio
SHOT 2 (2–4s) SETUP — MS tracking
camera_motion: handheld tracking behind her through the studio, chrome mirrors and shelves streaking past
action_visual: she drops into an ash-grey velvet stool. The esthetician snaps a white cape open and clicks it shut at her neck. A long emerald evening gown hangs soft-focus behind her; black leather ankle boots sit under the stool
exit: hands enter frame toward her face
SHOT 3 (4–6s) — insert macro on hands
camera_motion: static macro, 85mm
action_visual: a pump bottle pushes white cleansing foam into a gloved palm; gloved hands work the foam up her cheeks in one upward sweep. Wet foam squelch, pump hiss
exit: water enters frame
SHOT 4 (6–8s) — MCU
camera_motion: static, slight handheld float
action_visual: water rinses the foam, she lifts her wet face and droplets fly sideways catching the beauty-dish light
exit: a brush enters frame from the left
SHOT 5 (8–10s) SIGNATURE — MCU, frontal
vfx: none. Speed 100%
camera_motion: locked frontal, composition does not shift
action_visual: a brush lays charcoal clay mask in three strokes → jump to the mask already dry. She raises ONE eyebrow, the crust cracks across the bridge of her nose. The esthetician freezes mid-air with the brush. Aiko lowers the brow back
exit: two gel patches click under her eyes
SHOT 6 (10–12s) — MCU, frontal, same framing
camera_motion: locked, no movement
action_visual: the make-up artist slams a large powder puff straight into the lens, frame goes soft white → clears on glowing porcelain make-up and satin lips
exit: a brush sweeps across frame
SHOT 7 (12–14s) — MCU, same framing
camera_motion: locked
action_visual: the brush clears to reveal a sharp graphic black cat-eye liner and pale lips → a palm sweeps down the frame → clears on deep bronze eyelids
exit: the powder puff slams into the lens again
SHOT 8 (14–16s) — MCU, same framing
camera_motion: locked, tiny push-in
action_visual: frame clears on full smoky bronze eye, subtle highlighter on the cheekbones, deep berry-red lipstick. The make-up artist steps back and raises both hands. Aiko holds still half a second. Lipstick cap clicks
exit: a towel is pulled off her head
SHOT 9 (16–18s) — MS
camera_motion: static, then whip pan out and back
action_visual: the stylist rips the towel away, wet hair drops in one sheet → whip pan returns on a sleek glossy black bob shining under the beauty-dish light
exit: a flat iron enters frame
SHOT 10 (18–19s) — insert macro
vfx: speed ramp 50%→100%
camera_motion: static macro, 50mm
action_visual: the flat iron clicks open and releases a glossy sheet of hair, steam rises into the light beam
exit: steam crosses the lens
SHOT 11 (19–21s) — MS
camera_motion: two fast whip pans, out and back each time
action_visual: hair pushed back loose, the stylist tucks it behind her ear → whip pan → sleek blunt-bangs bob with a sharp edge. Aiko tips her head, hair swings across frame
exit: hair fills frame
SHOT 12 (21–24s) CLIMAX — MS to WS, studio floor
camera_motion: static, then slow pull-back
action_visual: the stylist lifts the emerald gown off a chrome hanger, the fabric flies across the whole frame — deep emerald floods the screen — then falls away: Aiko stands in the middle of the studio in a long emerald silk evening gown, bob restored, silk running to the floor. Silk whoosh, heavy fabric fall
exit: fingers rise to her ear
SHOT 13 (24–26s) — insert macro on ear
camera_motion: static macro, 85mm
action_visual: the make-up artist's fingers fasten a small emerald drop earring at her ear. The clasp click lands as the beat drop
exit: camera pulls wide
SHOT 14 (26–28s) — WS full length
camera_motion: static wide, three staff visible either side of her
action_visual: Aiko glances aside, takes two steps to the stool and bends down. Low insert: the emerald silk hem lifts and black leather ankle boots enter frame — one foot, then the other
exit: she straightens up
SHOT 15 (28–30s) PUNCHLINE — WS full length
camera_motion: locked off, no movement
action_visual: she straightens, adjusts the hem so the ankle boots stay visible, and looks straight into the lens. All three staff freeze. The stylist drops a brush — it clatters on the marble. One mouth corner lifts. She walks out of frame. Beat cuts to silence on the clatter
exit: empty studio, three women looking after her, brush on the floor. Hold one second, end
GENERATE FIRST: Shot 5 (eyebrow cracks the mask — the tone-setter), Shot 12 (emerald gown fabric wipe), Shot 15 (ankle boots punchline).
```

### Ultra-Real Personal Home Video of a Korean Woman

> Made with seedance 2.5 Prompt: Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video of a young Korean woman con

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/media/poster/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-personal-home-video-of-a-young-korean-906969cda347.jpg" width="600" alt="Ultra-Real Personal Home Video of a Korean Woman">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-personal-home-video-of-a-young-korean-906969cda347?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**Author:** @Ciri_ai | **Source:** [Original](https://x.com/Ciri_ai/status/2095026480552271884) | **Published:** 2026-09-02 | **Heat:** 69

**Stability:** 76/100

**Retest:** MiniMax H3 Max 768p · 2026-09-07 · ✅ reproduced (score 76) · [output](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-personal-home-video-of-a-young-korean-906969cda347/video-minimax-h3-768p-20260907-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (95 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video of a young Korean woman confidently attempting to learn a viral TikTok dance trend in her tiny apartment, with her furniture and coordination working against her the entire time. No reference image.

**MAIN SUBJECT**

Young Korean woman in her early 20s, casual and determined energy, natural makeup, expression that shifts from focused confidence to flustered embarrassment.

Hair in a messy half-up bun, a few strands sticking to her face as she gets more flustered, small stud earrings.

Wearing an oversized band t-shirt tucked loosely into biker shorts, white ankle socks, no shoes, phone propped up against a stack of books on the floor to "record for practice."

Maintain the same face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and appearance throughout the entire video.

**SETTING**

A small, cluttered studio apartment living room, evening, warm lamp light mixed with the cool glow of a laptop screen playing the dance tutorial video.

A coffee table pushed slightly out of the way but not far enough, a floor lamp standing a little too close to her "stage area," a pile of laundry on the couch, a half-full water glass on the side table, string lights on the wall, and a roommate's bike leaning against the wall in the background. Cramped, real, an apartment clearly not built for dancing.

**CAMERA / VISUAL AESTHETIC**

Raw personal footage, filmed by her roommate on a phone propped up at first, then grabbed handheld once things go wrong — strong handheld vlog shake for the second half.

Static, slightly low-angle framing for the "practice" shots, mimicking a phone-on-a-stack-of-books setup. Frame is imperfectly centered, cutting off the top of her head slightly. Once the roommate grabs the phone, autofocus hunts, motion blur kicks in during the collision moment, exposure shifts as she moves between lamp light and shadow, visible digital noise in the dim room.

No stabilization, no gimbal smoothness, no cinematic camera moves — it should feel like a real phone recording that was never meant to be watched by anyone but her.

---

**00:00–00:05 — THE CONFIDENT SETUP**

She props her phone against a stack of books, steps back into frame, and shakes out her arms like an athlete warming up.

She points at the laptop screen playing the dance tutorial, then back at herself, deadly serious.

*"Okay. I've watched this like twenty times. I got this."*

**00:05–00:10 — FIRST EIGHT-COUNT**

The music starts. She nails the first few moves surprisingly well, sharp and confident, clearly proud of herself mid-move.

Roommate's voice from off-screen, genuinely impressed: *"Wait, okay—?!"*

The camera stays static, catching her in full frame, a rare clean moment.

**00:10–00:15 — THE FIRST WOBBLE**

A quick turn in the choreography catches her off guard — she spins a half-second late, arms flailing to catch up, and clips the edge of the coffee table with her shin.

She yelps, hopping on one foot, still trying to keep the rhythm going through gritted teeth.

*"I'm fine — I'm fine, keep going—"*

**00:15–00:20 — LAMP INCIDENT**

Recovering, she throws herself into the next big arm movement — her hand clips the floor lamp, sending it tilting dangerously.

Her roommate lunges into frame to catch it, grabbing the phone off the books in the process, camera suddenly handheld and shaking.

*"THE LAMP—"*

**00:20–00:25 — FULL CHAOS**

With the lamp saved but the camera now shaky and handheld, she tries to pick the choreography back up from memory, increasingly out of sync with the music, arms going in directions the dance never intended.

She knocks the water glass off the side table with her elbow, doesn't even stop, just grimaces and keeps dancing through the spill.

Roommate, laughing hysterically behind the now-unsteady camera: *"You're literally destroying the apartment—"*

**00:25–00:30 — FINAL MOMENT**

The song ends. She strikes a triumphant final pose — chest heaving, one hand on her hip, completely out of breath, water pooling near her sock.

She looks into the camera, dead serious despite the disaster around her.

*"...Post it. That was clean."*

Her roommate, still laughing, pans the camera slowly down to the spilled water and tipped lamp before swinging back to her unbothered face — and the footage cuts abruptly to black.

---

**AUDIO**

Natural location sound only:

The tutorial/dance track playing tinny from the laptop speakers, her footsteps and fabric movement, the thud of her shin hitting the table, the lamp wobbling and roommate's scramble to catch it, glass tipping and water spilling, her roommate's genuine off-camera laughter throughout, and handheld camera noise once it's picked up.

No added music beyond the in-scene laptop track. No narration. No artificial sound effects.

Spoken lines: *"Okay. I've watched this like twenty times. I got this."* / *"Wait, okay—?!"* (roommate) / *"I'm fine — I'm fine, keep going—"* / *"THE LAMP—"* (roommate) / *"You're literally destroying the apartment—"* (roommate) / *"...Post it. That was clean."*

**FINAL FEEL**

Chaotic, cramped-apartment energy — the universal experience of confidently trying a viral dance and immediately losing a fight with your own furniture. Funny, physical, unscripted-feeling, with a deadpan ending that lands harder because she genuinely doesn't acknowledge the destruction around her.

```
One single continuous 20-second shot, 16:9 landscape, 1080p. NO cuts, NO edits, NO transitions, NO fades, NO title card, NO slow-motion. One unbroken handheld-gimbal camera that moves with the action the entire time. Photoreal supernatural school drama.

STORY
A quiet girl is cornered by four classmates in an old shrine courtyard behind her school. They tear out the pressed-flower pages she has been collecting in her journal and shove her down. Her hurt turns to resolve, and the wind itself answers her. She defeats the four one at a time with acrobatic moves that create visible gusts, calls her flying pages back out of the air, and walks away with them.

REFERENCES
@image1 = the heroine. One girl shown in two views (full body and face close-up); both views are the SAME person. Keep her exact face, dark teal-black layered shoulder-length hair with side-swept bangs, silver hairclip, brown eyes, small beauty mark, slim build.
@image2 = four antagonists, mapped left to right: A = tall, long straight black hair, center part (the leader); B = chestnut bob with blunt bangs; C = high black ponytail with white scrunchie; D = wavy dark-brown hair past the shoulders. Keep each face and hairstyle independent and consistent; never swap identities.
All five wear the reference uniform exactly: navy double-button blazer with white lapel trim, white shirt, red ribbon tie, plaid pleated knee-length skirt, black tights, brown loafers. No other clothing. Do NOT show the white studio backgrounds, lineup poses or split-screen layouts from the references. All five people are the same real-world scale.

NO DISAPPEARING (CRITICAL)
Exactly five real people exist in this scene from the first frame to the last frame: the heroine, A, B, C and D. NOBODY vanishes, fades, dissolves, turns into leaves or dust, teleports, gets blown out of the scene, or is swallowed by the wind. Wind never removes a person from the shot. When the camera moves away from a character and returns, she is still exactly where she was left. Every defeated girl stays physically on the ground in her defeated position, visible, solid and breathing, until the very last frame. Nobody walks or runs out of the courtyard. The count of people stays at five at all times.

SETTING
An old shrine courtyard behind a high school, late-afternoon golden light, soft overcast sky. Gravel and stone-slab ground, two moss-covered stone lanterns, a small wooden shrine gate (torii) in the background, a large ginkgo tree dropping yellow leaves. A low wooden bench on the left. Everything is flat and open, no stairs, no drops, no water. The wind is NOT a storm: it is a controlled, localized swirl of a limited number of yellow ginkgo leaves, light gravel dust and moving hair. The leaves stay thin and sparse, never form a thick cloud or wall, and never cover a person's body or face. Wind is transparent: no glowing beams, no neon, no smoke, no animal shapes, no tornado.

PROPS
One small hand-bound journal with a green cloth cover. Exactly five thin washi-paper pages, each holding a pressed flower (a pink cosmos, a blue hydrangea, a red maple leaf, a tiny forget-me-not, a white camellia), are torn out together. The journal is dropped near the heroine's feet and stays there until she picks it up. The five pages float in the air at the left side of the courtyard during the fight and never act as weapons. Two school bags rest against the shrine wall from the start and are never touched.

CAMERA, ONE CONTINUOUS MOVE
The camera starts in a tight handheld medium shot on the heroine's face, drifts around her, then follows each action in a smooth flowing path at eye level and side three-quarter angles, mostly medium to medium-full so every acrobatic move is fully visible. It never cuts. It sweeps from character to character with whip-pan-style swings that settle on the next target. After each defeat, the camera glides briefly across the fallen girl so the audience sees she is still there. At the climax the camera does one 100-degree orbit around the heroine at normal speed. In the final seconds the camera slowly pulls back to a medium-wide view that shows all five people.

PERFORMANCE TIMELINE (all in one take)

0–4s: Camera starts close on the heroine, holding her green journal against her chest. A (long black hair) snatches it, holds it out of reach and says in a mocking voice, in English: "This is trash." She tears five pressed-flower pages out in one handful and flings them into the air while the others laugh. Camera pans with the pages fluttering, then returns.

4–7s: She turns to chase the pages. B (chestnut bob) steps in and shoves her; she stumbles, drops to one knee and one palm, the journal falls near her. A few loose ginkgo leaves skitter across the gravel. Camera pushes in on her wet eyes and tight jaw. Laughter fades. Hold the hurt for a beat. A, B, C and D are all visible standing nearby.

7–9s: She says quietly and firmly, in English: "Stop it already." Her hair lifts, a few leaves begin to circle, and the five pages stop and hover on the left. The four step back, uneasy, all clearly visible. B moves in first.

9–12s: DEFEAT 1, B. The heroine pushes off her palm into a single handspring into a side cartwheel. Her spinning legs pull the air into one narrow gust that travels sideways across the gravel into B's chest. B is thrown off balance, slides, and ends seated against the left stone lantern base. She stays there, visible, for the rest of the video.

12–16s: DEFEAT 2, C. The camera glances at B still seated, then whips to C, who charges from the right. The heroine drops into a low crouch and slides on the gravel into one long circular leg sweep. The sweep sends a flat current of leaves and dust along the ground into C's ankles. C's feet slip and she falls forward onto her hands and knees at the right edge. She stays there, visible.

16–20s: DEFEAT 3, D. D rushes in with both hands out. The heroine springs up, performs one standing back handspring into a backflip. As she rotates, her heels drag a wide, flat wall of air in front of her that hits D at torso height. D is pushed back two steps and sits down hard on the wooden bench. She stays seated, visible. Nobody collides with anyone else.

DEFEAT POSITIONS (never reset)
B: seated at the left stone lantern. C: hands and knees at the right edge. D: seated on the bench. A: seated alone near the torii gate. All four stay conscious, remain in the courtyard, never rejoin the fight and are never hit by another person's attack.

RULES FOR THE POWERS
Every technique follows: acrobatic movement, wind forms, wind travels, wind makes contact, reaction. The heroine's feet never touch anyone. Each gust fades after its own hit. Only the targeted person is affected; the others may flinch. No blades of air, no blood, no injuries, no one hits their head. One execution per move: no repeated flips, no extra spins.

AUDIO
Natural courtyard ambience, distant cicadas, rustling leaves, gravel scrapes, shoes, paper tearing, mocking laughter, breathing, cloth movement, wind whooshes, bodies landing on gravel. Only TWO spoken lines in the whole video, both in clear natural English with accurate lip sync: A says "This is trash." and the heroine says "Stop it already." No other dialogue. No music, no narrator, no subtitles, no on-screen text.

LOOK
Natural daylight color with warm golden tones, fine film grain, soft halation, real skin texture, no plastic faces, no HDR glow, no vignette, no letterboxing. Side-on, athletic stunt framing; clothing moves naturally with the acrobatics, never framed as spectacle.

AVOID
Anyone disappearing, fading, dissolving, teleporting or leaving the courtyard; a storm, tornado or thick leaf cloud; cuts, split-screens, studio backgrounds, duplicated heroine, extra characters, group takedowns, changed uniforms, mint or green hair on anyone, sliding planted feet, extra limbs, extra pages (exactly five), extra dialogue, music, subtitles, logos.

END
Exactly 20.0 seconds in one unbroken take. The heroine stands holding her journal with all five pressed-flower pages recovered. All four defeated girls are visibly present in their separate positions. Two bags remain by the shrine wall.
```

Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI

Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic early-2000s DV home video of a 20-year-old Japanese woman spending a quiet late-summer afternoon in an old seaside neighborhood in Kamakura, Japan.

A 20-year-old Japanese woman, naturally beautiful, realistic skin texture, minimal makeup, long dark-brown wavy hair loosely tied with a simple fabric hair tie, wearing a fitted pale-yellow short-sleeve top, loose light-blue wide-leg trousers, white canvas sneakers, and a delicate silver necklace. Keep her identity, facial features, outfit, hairstyle, body proportions, and age perfectly consistent throughout the entire video.

She leaves a small traditional Japanese rental house carrying a woven shoulder tote bag and walks through a narrow residential lane toward a tiny local bakery near the coast.

**SCENE 1 — LEAVING HOME**

She steps outside, slides the wooden door closed behind her, adjusts her tote bag and walks down a quiet narrow street lined with old Japanese houses, bicycles, potted plants, utility poles and overhead wires.

She looks toward the camera and casually says:

**“I finally have a slow afternoon.”**

The camera follows her from behind with imperfect handheld movement.

**SCENE 2 — LOCAL BAKERY**

She enters a tiny old-fashioned neighborhood bakery with a wooden entrance and glass display case.

She chooses a melon pan, a small red-bean pastry and a bottled ramune. She places everything carefully inside her woven tote bag.

While looking through the small refrigerator, she notices several colorful popsicles.

She picks up two peach-flavored popsicles, smiles and adds them to her bag.

The bakery owner places the items into a small paper package and hands it to her.

She politely says:

**“Thank you, I’ll come again.”**

**SCENE 3 — SEASIDE STREET**

Outside, she walks toward the coast.

She notices two neighborhood children sitting near a low stone wall, watching bicycles pass by.

She takes the two peach popsicles from her bag and gives one to each child.

Each child clearly receives one and happily holds it.

One child says:

**“Really? Thank you!”**

She smiles and replies:

**“Enjoy it before it melts.”**

The children happily begin eating their popsicles.

The popsicles visibly transfer from the bakery refrigerator → woman's tote bag → children's hands. No disappearing or duplicated objects.

**SCENE 4 — BICYCLE**

As she continues walking, she notices one child's bicycle has fallen over beside the sidewalk.

She stops, picks it up and holds the handlebars while the child checks the bicycle.

The child smiles and says:

**“You helped me!”**

She laughs softly and replies:

**“No problem. Ride carefully.”**

The child rides away with the other child.

**SCENE 5 — WATER FOUNTAIN**

She continues toward the seaside and discovers an old public water fountain beside a small park.

She turns the metal tap.

Water begins flowing naturally.

She cups her hands under the water and splashes cool water onto her face.

She laughs naturally, shakes the water from her hands and wipes her face.

She looks refreshed and says:

**“That feels so good.”**

**SCENE 6 — WALKING HOME**

The late-afternoon sunlight falls across the quiet Japanese street.

She walks back toward her house carrying the same woven tote bag.

A bicycle bell rings somewhere in the distance.

She briefly looks directly into the DV camera and says:

**“Best kind of afternoon.”**

She smiles, turns toward her house and walks through the wooden entrance.

The camera remains outside for a moment as the door closes.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

**CAMERA / VISUAL STYLE**

Raw handheld early-2000s Japanese DV camcorder footage. Authentic consumer camcorder aesthetic, shaky handheld movement, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, occasional focus breathing, faded slightly washed-out colors, soft digital detail, mild compression artifacts, exposure shifts

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video showing an ordinary Sunday morning in the life of a young Korean woman. No reference image.

MAIN SUBJECT

Young Korean woman in her early 20s, natural and approachable look, realistic skin texture with visible pores, bare-faced or barely-there makeup, warm and slightly playful expression.

Shoulder-length wavy black hair left down, side-swept bangs, small mole near her left eye.

Wearing an oversized pastel-yellow hoodie, loose gray jogger pants, fuzzy slide sandals, and a small crossbody pouch bag.

Maintain the same face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and appearance throughout the entire video.

SETTING

A small countryside town on a bright, breezy Sunday morning.

A traditional outdoor market with striped awnings, crates of vegetables, a fishmonger's stall, an old bus stop with a rusted bench, a narrow road running alongside rice paddies, a local bakery with a hand-painted sign, a grandmother's front yard with drying red peppers on a mat, chickens wandering nearby, and mountains faintly visible in the background. Everything sun-bleached, dusty, and authentically rural.

CAMERA / VISUAL AESTHETIC

Raw personal footage casually recorded by a friend, strong handheld vlog feel throughout.

Constant natural hand shake and micro-jitters, footsteps visibly bouncing the frame, imperfect and often off-center framing, occasional partial crops of her face or shoulder, autofocus visibly hunting between her and the background, exposure flaring when the camera swings toward the sun, mild motion blur on quick turns, soft image detail, subtle digital noise, slightly warm and faded color grading like an old phone camera.

The camera operator walks and jogs to keep up with her, breathing audible at times, camera dipping and swaying with each step.

No stabilization, no gimbal smoothness, no cinematic camera movements, no dramatic lighting, no slow motion, no polished commercial appearance.

The footage should feel like a real, unplanned phone recording — accidentally beautiful rather than deliberately cinematic.

---

00:00–00:05 — MARKET ENTRANCE

She weaves through the outdoor market stalls, the camera bouncing behind her as it struggles to keep pace.

She stops briefly to pick up a small bag of cherry tomatoes, holding them up toward the camera with a grin.

The frame jolts as the operator steps over a crate in the path.

**00:05–00:10 — FISH STALL DETOUR**

She wanders to the fishmonger's stall, wrinkling her nose playfully at the smell and laughing.

The camera whip-pans slightly too fast trying to follow her hand gesture, briefly blurring before catching focus again.

She waves the camera away, still laughing: *"Ugh, don't film that, film me!"*

**00:10–00:15 — BUS STOP WAIT**

She jogs to catch the local bus stop bench just as an old bus pulls away without her, the camera shaking hard from the sudden running motion.

She throws her hands up in mock frustration, plops down on the rusted bench, and sighs dramatically, laughing at herself.

The frame settles slightly off-center as she rests her chin on her palm.

**00:15–00:20 — RICE PADDY ROAD**

She walks along the narrow road beside the rice paddies, wind blowing loose strands across her face.

The camera drifts low near her feet as she kicks a small pebble, then swings back up to her face, slightly overexposed from the open sky.

She spins around once with her arms out, the frame swaying and overshooting past her before correcting.

**00:20–00:25 — GRANDMOTHER'S YARD**

She ducks into a yard where red peppers dry on a mat, crouching to gently shoo away a curious chicken pecking near her sandals.

The camera jerks back suddenly as the chicken flaps its wings, both of them startled and laughing.

She picks up a dried pepper, sniffs it, makes a face, and tosses it back down.

**00:25–00:30 — FINAL MOMENT**

She grabs a warm bag of fresh bread from the nearby bakery counter, takes a big bite, and turns straight to the camera mid-chew.

The frame shakes as she leans in too close, filling half the screen with her grinning face.

She says, muffled and amused:

*"Okay, stop filming me eating!"*

She swats the camera lens with her hand, and the footage cuts abruptly to black, mid-laugh.

---

**AUDIO**

Natural location sound only:

Market chatter, footsteps crunching on gravel, a bus engine pulling away, wind, chickens clucking, rustling plastic bags, distant rooster crow, bread bag crinkling, the operator's breathing and quiet laughter, and audible handheld camera noise.

No music. No narration. No artificial sound effects.

Only the spoken lines: *"Ugh, don't film that, film me!"* and *"Okay, stop filming me eating!"*

**FINAL FEEL**

The entire video should feel like a chaotic, cheerful, completely unplanned Sunday morning caught on a friend's phone — bright, breezy, playful, and warmly nostalgic.

No dramatic events. No exaggerated acting. No model poses. No cinematic hero shots.

The realism should come from constant handheld imperfection, sun glare, dust, uneven pacing, real laughter, and moments that feel genuinely caught rather than staged.
```
